{"etymology_number":"4","forms":[{"form":"ringers","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Any person or thing that is fraudulent; a fake or impostor."],"tags":["slang"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In this next clip from the security camera, we see that the rube flashing his wad of cash thinks he'll win, but he doesn't realize that the guy in the gray shirt is a ringer.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person highly proficient at a skill or sport who is brought in, often fraudulently, to supplement a team."],"synonyms":[{"word":"hustler"}],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"glosses":["A horse fraudulently entered in a race using the name of another horse."],"topics":["hobbies","horse-racing","horseracing","horses","lifestyle","pets","racing","sports"]},{"glosses":["A fraudulently cloned (or cut-and-shut) motor vehicle."],"tags":["UK","slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹɪŋə(ɹ)/"}],"upstream_line":241283,"upstream_offset":1198127697,"upstream_record_sha256":"0bae930bcc2d9df44da286bd2749a0c8505b4dda665fb18b8c64349ead080bcf","word":"ringer"}
{"etymology_number":"5","forms":[{"form":"ringers","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"That man over there is an exact ringer for my father!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person, animal, or entity which resembles another so closely as to be taken for the other; a look-alike (now usually in the phrase dead ringer)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"dead ringer"}]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹɪŋə(ɹ)/"}],"upstream_line":241284,"upstream_offset":1198131016,"upstream_record_sha256":"90f74c6b612b02f9c8cc8936d6348d1eeadd3632c09bf9133cbf03d0fd150e36","word":"ringer"}
